# Graph Report - AI-OS  (2026-07-31)

## Corpus Check
- 1186 files · ~3,197,325 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 17100 nodes · 26867 edges · 1279 communities (1091 shown, 188 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 713 edges (avg confidence: 0.74)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `69bd3725`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
